# Citation review queue — PROPOSE ONLY, nothing here has been written into the corpus

Generated by `build/resolve_citations.js` over 489 of 489 `evi.trials[].ref` strings.

**A proposal is not a citation.** Each row below needs a human to confirm the paper actually
supports the finding it is attached to — matching an author and a year does not mean the paper
says what the claim says. Reject freely; a missing citation is recoverable and a wrong one is not.

| bucket | count | share |
|---|---|---|
| auto-resolved (single hit on author+year) | 136 | 27.8% |
| ambiguous (needs a human to pick) | 317 | 64.8% |
| structurally unresolvable (rewrite the ref first) | 36 | 7.4% |
| errored | 0 | 0.0% |
